<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ أَبِي صَعْصَعَةَ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ عَمْرَو بْنَ الْجَمُوحِ، وَعَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عَمْرٍو الأَنْصَارِيَّيْنِ، ثُمَّ السَّلَمِيَّيْنِ كَانَا قَدْ حَفَرَ السَّيْلُ قَبْرَهُمَا وَكَانَ قَبْرُهُمَا مِمَّا يَلِي السَّيْلَ وَكَانَا فِي قَبْرٍ وَاحِدٍ وَهُمَا مِمَّنِ اسْتُشْهِدَ يَوْمَ أُحُدٍ فَحُفِرَ عَنْهُمَا لِيُغَيَّرَا مِنْ مَكَانِهِمَا فَوُجِدَا لَمْ يَتَغَيَّرَا كَأَنَّهُمَا مَاتَا بِالأَمْسِ وَكَانَ أَحَدُهُمَا قَدْ جُرِحَ فَوَضَعَ يَدَهُ عَلَى جُرْحِهِ فَدُفِنَ وَهُوَ كَذَلِكَ فَأُمِيطَتْ يَدُهُ عَنْ جُرْحِهِ ثُمَّ أُرْسِلَتْ فَرَجَعَتْ كَمَا كَانَتْ وَكَانَ بَيْنَ أُحُدٍ وَبَيْنَ يَوْمَ حُفِرَ عَنْهُمَا سِتٌّ وَأَرْبَعُونَ سَنَةً ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ بَأْسَ أَنْ يُدْفَنَ الرَّجُلاَنِ وَالثَّلاَثَةُ فِي قَبْرٍ وَاحِدٍ مِنْ ضَرُورَةٍ وَيُجْعَلَ الأَكْبَرُ مِمَّا يَلِي الْقِبْلَةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Amr ibn al-Jamuh al-Ansari (companion); Abdullah ibn Umar al-Ansari (unresolved). The exact source describes two named Uhud graves being uncovered. The second printed personal name is retained without correction; preceding and following compiler legal discussion remains context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abd ar-Rahman ibn Abi Sasaca that he had heard that Amr ibn al-Jamuh al-Ansari and Abdullah ibn Umar al-Ansari, both of the tribe of Banu Salami, had their grave uncovered by a flood. Their grave was part of what was left after the flood. They were in the same grave, and they were among those martyred at Uhud. They were dug up so that they might be moved. They were found unchanged. It was as if they had died only the day before. One of them had been wounded, and he had put his hand over his wound and had been buried like that. His hand was pulled away from his wound and released, and it returned to where it had been. It was forty-six years between Uhud and the day they were dug up.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1007]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1007</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1007 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Malik was asked whether, when an imam had accepted jizya from a people and they gave it, he thought that the land of one of them who surrendered belonged to him or whether his land and property belonged to the Muslims. Malik said, &quot;That varies. As for the people of peace, if one of them surrenders, then he is entitled to his land and property. As for the people of force who use force, if one of them surrenders, his land and property belong to the Muslims because the people of force are overcome in their towns, and it becomes booty for the Muslims. As for the people of peace, their property and selves are protected so that they make peace for them. Only what they have made peace for is obliged of them.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;There is no harm in burying two or three men in the same grave due to necessity. The oldest one is put next to the qibla</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुर्रहमान बिन अबी सासा से रिवायत है कि अम्र बिन जमुह और अब्दुर्रहमान बिन अम्र अंसारी सलमी जो शहीद हुए थे जंग-ए-उहुद में, उनकी क़ब्र को पानी के बहाव ने उखेड़ दिया था और क़ब्र उनकी बहाव के नज़दीक थी और दोनों एक ही क़ब्र में थे। तो क़ब्र खोदी गई तो लाशें वैसी ही थीं जैसे वो शहीद हुए थे, गोया कल मरे हैं। उन में से एक शख्श को जब ज़ख़्म लगा था तो उसने हाथ अपने ज़ख़्म पर रख लिया था, जब उनको दफ़न करने लगे तो हाथ वहाँ से हटाया मगर हाथ पर वहीं आ लगा। जब उनकी लाशें खोदें तो जंग-ए-उहुद को छयालीस बरस गुज़र चुके थे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبدالرحمن بن ابی صعصہ سے روایت ہے کہ عمرو بن الجموع اور عبدالرحمن بن عمرو انصار سلمی جو شہید ہوئے تھے جنگ احد میں ان کی قبر کو پانی کے بہاؤ نے اکھیڑ دیا تھا اور قبر ان کی بہاؤ کے نزدیک تھی اور دونوں ایک ہی قبر میں تھے تو قبر کھودی گئی تو لاشیں ویسی ہی تہیں جیسے وہ شہید ہوئے تھے گویا کل مرے ہیں ان میں سے ایک شخص کو جب زخم لگا تھا تو اس نے ہاتھ اپنے زخم پر رکھ لیا تھا جب ان کو دفن کرنے لگے تو ہاتھ وہاں سے ہٹایا مگر ہاتھ پر وہیں آلگا جب ان کی لاشیں کھو دیں تو جنگ احد کو چھیالیس برس گزر چکے تھے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdurrahman bin Abi Sasa se riwayat hai ki Amr bin Jamuh aur Abdurrahman bin Amr Ansari Salmi jo shaheed huye the jang-e-uhud mein, unki qabr ko pani ke bahao ne ukhed diya tha aur qabr unki bahao ke nazdeek thi aur dono ek hi qabr mein the. Toh qabr khodi gayi toh lashein waisi hi thin jaise wo shaheed huye the, goya kal mare hain. Un mein se ek shakhs ko jab zakhm laga tha toh usne hath apne zakhm par rakh liya tha, jab unko dafan karne lage toh hath wahan se hataya magar hath par wahin aa laga. Jab unki lashein khodin toh jang-e-uhud ko chhayalees baras guzar chuke the.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1007" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ قَدِمَ عَلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ مَالٌ مِنَ الْبَحْرَيْنِ فَقَالَ مَنْ كَانَ لَهُ عِنْدَ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَأْىٌ أَوْ عِدَةٌ فَلْيَأْتِنِي فَجَاءَهُ جَابِرُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ فَحَفَنَ لَهُ ثَلاَثَ حَفَنَاتٍ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Bakr as-Siddiq (companion); Jabir ibn Abdullah (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman said, &quot;Property was sent to Abu Bakr as-Siddiq from Bahrayn. He said, &#x27;If someone had a promise or a pledge with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, let him come to me.&#x27; So Jabir ibn Abdullah came to him, and he gave him three times as much of it as would fill both hands</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1008]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1008" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">राबिआ बिन आबि अब्दुर्रहमान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि हज़रत अबू बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास रूपया आया बहरीन से. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने मुनादी कराई की जिस से रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने कुछ देने का वादा किया हो वो हमारे पास आये. जाबिर बिन अब्दुल्लाह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) आये अन्हु) ने उनको तीन लैप भर कर दिए.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1008" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1003

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1009)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، أَنَّ سَعْدَ بْنَ عُبَادَةَ، اسْتَفْتَى رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ إِنَّ أُمِّي مَاتَتْ وَعَلَيْهَا نَذْرٌ وَلَمْ تَقْضِهِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ اقْضِهِ عَنْهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1009]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1009" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1009</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1009 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Ubaydullah ibn Abdullah ibn Utba ibn Masud from Abdullah ibn Abbas that Sad ibn Ubada questioned the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, and said,&quot;My mother died while she still had a vow which she had not fulfilled.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Fulfill it for her</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1009]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1009" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) से रिवायत है कि साद बिन उबादा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने पूछा रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से कि, &#x27;मेरी माँ मर गई और उस पर एक नज़्र वाजिब थी, उसने अदा नहीं की।&#x27; आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया, &#x27;तो अदा करो उसकी तरफ़ से।&#x27;</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1009]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1009" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ سعد بن عبادہ نے پوچھا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے کہ میری ماں مر گئی اور اس پر ایک نذر واجب تھی اس نے ادا نہیں کی آپ نے فرمایا تو ادا کرو اس کی طرف سے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1009]</p>

## Narration 1004

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي بَكْرٍ، عَنْ عَمَّتِهِ، أَنَّهَا حَدَّثَتْهُ عَنْ جَدَّتِهِ، أَنَّهَا كَانَتْ جَعَلَتْ عَلَى نَفْسِهَا مَشْيًا إِلَى مَسْجِدِ قُبَاءٍ فَمَاتَتْ وَلَمْ تَقْضِهِ فَأَفْتَى عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبَّاسٍ ابْنَتَهَا أَنْ تَمْشِيَ عَنْهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى وَسَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ لاَ يَمْشِي أَحَدٌ عَنْ أَحَدٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1010" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdullah ibn Abi Bakr that his paternal aunt related that her grandmother made a vow to walk to the Quba mosque. She died, and did not fulfill it, so Abdullah ibn Abbas asked her daughter to walk for her.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1010]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1010" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1010</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1010 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he had heard Malik say, &quot;No one walks for anyone else</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1010" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1010]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1010" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1010</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1010 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अबी बक्र से रिवायत है कि उन्होंने सुना अपनी फुफी से, उन्होंने बयान किया कि उनकी दादी ने नज़्र की मस्जिद-ए-कुबा में पैदल जाने की, फिर मर गईं और उस नज़्र को अदा नहीं किया, तो अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) ने उनकी बेटी को हुक्म किया कि वो उनकी तरफ़ से इस नज़्र को अदा करें।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1010" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1005

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1011]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1011</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1011 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ أَبِي حَبِيبَةَ، قَالَ قُلْتُ لِرَجُلٍ وَأَنَا حَدِيثُ السِّنِّ، مَا عَلَى الرَّجُلِ أَنْ يَقُولَ عَلَىَّ مَشْىٌ إِلَى بَيْتِ اللَّهِ وَلَمْ يَقُلْ عَلَىَّ نَذْرُ مَشْىٍ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ لِي رَجُلٌ هَلْ لَكَ أَنْ أُعْطِيَكَ هَذَا الْجِرْوَ - لِجِرْوِ قِثَّاءٍ فِي يَدِهِ - وَتَقُولُ عَلَىَّ مَشْىٌ إِلَى بَيْتِ اللَّهِ قَالَ فَقُلْتُ نَعَمْ فَقُلْتُهُ وَأَنَا يَوْمَئِذٍ حَدِيثُ السِّنِّ ثُمَّ مَكَثْتُ حَتَّى عَقَلْتُ فَقِيلَ لِي إِنَّ عَلَيْكَ مَشْيًا فَجِئْتُ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ فَسَأَلْتُهُ عَنْ ذَلِكَ فَقَالَ لِي عَلَيْكَ مَشْىٌ ‏.‏ فَمَشَيْتُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَهَذَا الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abi Habiba (unresolved); Said ibn al- Musayyab (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Abdullah ibn Abi Habiba said, &quot;I said to a man, when I was young, &#x27;A man who only says that he must walk to the House of Allah and does not say that he has vowed to walk, does not have to walk.&#x27; A man said, &#x27;Shall I give you this small cucumber?&#x27; and he had a small cucumber in his hand and you will say, &#x27;I must walk to the house of Allah?&#x27; I said, &#x27;Yes&#x27; and I said it, for at that time I was still immature. Then, when I came of age, some one said to me that I had to fulfill my vow. I went and asked Said ibn al- Musayyab about it, and he said to me, &#x27;You must walk.&#x27; So I walked.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1011]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन आबि हबिबाह से रिवायत है के मैं ने एक शख्स से कहा और मैं काम-सीन था के अगर कोई शख्स सिर्फ इतना ही कहे के &#x27;अली मश्यन इला बैत-उल्लाह&#x27; यानी &#x27;मेरे ऊपर पैदल चलना है बैत-ुल्लाह तक&#x27; और ये नहीं कहा के &#x27;मेरे ऊपर नज़र है पैदल चलने की बैत-ुल्लाह तक&#x27; तो उस पर कुछ लाज़िम नहीं आता. वो शख्स मुझ से बोला के मेरे हाथ में ये काकरी है तुझे देता हूँ तू इतना कह दे के &#x27;मेरे ऊपर पैदल चलना है बैत-ुल्लाह तक&#x27; तो मैंने कह दिया और मैं काम-सीन था. फिर ठहर कर थोड़ी देर में मुझे अकल आई और लोगों ने मुझ से कहा के तुझ पर पैदल चलना बैत-ुल्लाह तक वाजिब हुआ तुझ पर पैदल चलना वाजिब हुआ बैत-ुल्लाह तक</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1011 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن ابی حبیبہ سے روایت ہے کہ میں نے کہا ایک شخص سے اور میں کمسن تھا کہ اگر کوئی شخص صرف اتنا ہی کہے کہ علی مشئی الی بیت اللہ یعنی میرے اوپر پیدل چلنا ہے بیت اللہ تک اور یہ نہیں کہا کہ میرے اوپر نذر ہے پیدل چلنے کی بیت اللہ تک تو اس پر کچھ لازم نہیں آتا، وہ شخص مجھ سے بولا کہ میرے ہاتھ میں یہ ککڑی ہے تجھے دیتا ہوں تو اتنا کہہ دے کہ میرے اوپر پیدل چلنا ہے بیت اللہ تک میں نے کہا ہاں کہتا ہوں تو میں نے کہہ دیا اور میں کم سن تھا پھر ٹھہر کر تھوڑی دیر میں مجھے عقل آئی اور لوگوں نے مجھ سے کہا کہ تجھ پیدل چلنا بیت اللہ تک واجب ہوا میں سعید بن مسیب کے پاس آیا اور ان سے پوچھا انہوں نے بھی کہا کہ تجھ پر پیدل چلنا واجب ہوا بیت اللہ تک تو میں پیدل چلا بیت اللہ تک ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1011 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abi Habibah se riwayat hai ke main ne ek shakhs se kaha aur main kam-sin tha ke agar koi shakhs sirf itna hi kahe ke &#x27;Ali mashyun ila bait-ullah&#x27; yaani &#x27;mere upar paidal chalna hai bait-ullah tak&#x27; aur ye nahi kaha ke &#x27;mere upar nazr hai paidal chalne ki bait-ullah tak&#x27; to us par kuch lazim nahi aata. Wo shakhs mujh se bola ke mere hath mein ye kakri hai tujhe deta hoon tu itna keh de ke &#x27;mere upar paidal chalna hai bait-ullah tak&#x27;, maine kaha haan kehta hoon to maine keh diya aur main kam-sin tha. Phir theher kar thodi der mein mujhe aqal aayi aur logon ne mujh se kaha ke tujh par paidal chalna bait-ullah tak wajib hua, main Saeed bin Musayyib ke paas aaya aur un se poocha, unhone bhi kaha ke tujh par paidal chalna wajib hua bait-ullah tak, to main paidal chala bait-ullah tak.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1011" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عُرْوَةَ بْنِ أُذَيْنَةَ اللَّيْثِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ خَرَجْتُ مَعَ جَدَّةٍ لِي عَلَيْهَا مَشْىٌ إِلَى بَيْتِ اللَّهِ حَتَّى إِذَا كُنَّا بِبَعْضِ الطَّرِيقِ عَجَزَتْ فَأَرْسَلَتْ مَوْلًى لَهَا يَسْأَلُ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ فَخَرَجْتُ مَعَهُ فَسَأَلَ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ فَقَالَ لَهُ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ مُرْهَا فَلْتَرْكَبْ ثُمَّ لْتَمْشِي مِنْ حَيْثُ عَجَزَتْ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى وَسَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ وَنَرَى عَلَيْهَا مَعَ ذَلِكَ الْهَدْىَ ‏.‏ وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ سَعِيدَ بْنَ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَأَبَا، سَلَمَةَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ كَانَا يَقُولاَنِ مِثْلَ قَوْلِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ ‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1012" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words. Report type: abbreviated transmission. Attribution: Said ibn al-Musayyab (successor); Abu Salama ibn Abd ar- Rahman (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Urwa ibn Udhayna al-Laythi said, &quot;I went out with my grandmother who had vowed to walk to the House of Allah. When we had gone part of the way, she could not go on. I sent one of her mawlas to question Abdullah ibn Umar and I went with him. He asked Abdullah ibn Umar, and Abdullah ibn Umar said to him, &#x27;Take her and let her ride, and when she has the strength let her ride back, and start to walk from the place from which she was unable to go on.&#x27;~</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Said ibn al-Musayyab and Abu Salama ibn Abd ar- Rahman said the same as Abdullah ibn Umar</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1012]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">उरवह बिन उज़ायनः लेथि से रिवायत है के कहा मैं निकला अपनी दादी के साथ और उसने नज़र की थी बैत-ुल्लाह तक पैदल जाने की मैं भी साथ गया. उसने अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से पुछा उन्होंने जवाब दिया कि अब सवार हो जाये फिर दोबारा जब आये जहाँ से सवार हुई थीं वहां से पैदल चले. कहा मालिक ने और बावजूद इसके एक हदया (क़ुरबानी) भी उस पर वाजिब है. सईद बिन मुसय्यब और ए.बी.ए. सलमः बिन अब्दुर-रहमान कहते थे इस मसले में जैसा अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1012" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عروہ بن اذینه لیثی سے روایت ہے کہ کہا میں نکلا اپنی دادی کے ساتھ اور اس نے نذر کی تھی بیت اللہ تک پیدل جانے کی، راستے میں تھک گئیں تو اپنے غلام کو بھیجا عبداللہ بن عمر کے پاس مسئلہ پوچھنے کو میں بھی ساتھ گیا اس نے عبداللہ بن عمر سے پوچھا انہوں نے جواب دیا کہ اب سوار ہو جائے پھر دوبارہ جب آئے جہاں سے سوار ہوئی تھیں وہاں سے پیدل چلے ۔ کہا مالک نے اور باوجود اس کے ایک ہدیہ بھی اس پر واجب ہے ۔ سعید بن مسیب اور ابا سلمہ بن عبدالرحمن کہتے تھے اس مسئلہ میں جیسا عبداللہ بن عمر نے کہا ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1012" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Urwah bin Uzaynah Laithi se riwayat hai ke kaha main nikla apni dadi ke sath aur usne nazr ki thi bait-ullah tak paidal jaane ki, raaste mein thak gayin to apne ghulam ko bheja Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas masla poochne ko, main bhi sath gaya. Usne Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se poocha unhone jawab diya ke ab sawar ho jaye phir dobara jab aaye jahan se sawar hui thin wahan se paidal chale. Kaha Malik ne aur bawajood iske ek hadya (qurbani) bhi us par wajib hai. Saeed bin Musayyib aur Aba Salamah bin Abdur-Rahman kehte thay is masle mein jaisa Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1012" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1007

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1014]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1014" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1014</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1014 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ كَانَ عَلَىَّ مَشْىٌ فَأَصَابَتْنِي خَاصِرَةٌ فَرَكِبْتُ حَتَّى أَتَيْتُ مَكَّةَ فَسَأَلْتُ عَطَاءَ بْنَ أَبِي رَبَاحٍ وَغَيْرَهُ فَقَالُوا عَلَيْكَ هَدْىٌ ‏.‏ فَلَمَّا قَدِمْتُ الْمَدِينَةَ سَأَلْتُ عُلَمَاءَهَا فَأَمَرُونِي أَنْ أَمْشِيَ مَرَّةً أُخْرَى مِنْ حَيْثُ عَجَزْتُ فَمَشَيْتُ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى وَسَمِعْتُ مَالِكًا يَقُولُ فَالأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِيمَنْ يَقُولُ عَلَىَّ مَشْىٌ إِلَى بَيْتِ اللَّهِ أَنَّهُ إِذَا عَجَزَ رَكِبَ ثُمَّ عَادَ فَمَشَى مِنْ حَيْثُ عَجَزَ فَإِنْ كَانَ لاَ يَسْتَطِيعُ الْمَشْىَ فَلْيَمْشِ مَا قَدَرَ عَلَيْهِ ثُمَّ لْيَرْكَبْ وَعَلَيْهِ هَدْىُ بَدَنَةٍ أَوْ بَقَرَةٍ أَوْ شَاةٍ إِنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ إِلاَّ هِيَ ‏.‏ وَسُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ يَقُولُ لِلرَّجُلِ أَنَا أَحْمِلُكَ إِلَى بَيْتِ اللَّهِ فَقَالَ مَالِكٌ إِنْ نَوَى أَنْ يَحْمِلَهُ عَلَى رَقَبَتِهِ يُرِيدُ بِذَلِكَ الْمَشَقَّةَ وَتَعَبَ نَفْسِهِ فَلَيْسَ ذَلِكَ عَلَيْهِ وَلْيَمْشِ عَلَى رِجْلَيْهِ وَلْيُهْدِ وَإِنْ لَمْ يَكُنْ نَوَى شَيْئًا فَلْيَحْجُجْ وَلْيَرْكَبْ وَلْيَحْجُجْ بِذَلِكَ الرَّجُلِ مَعَهُ وَذَلِكَ أَنَّهُ قَالَ أَنَا أَحْمِلُكَ إِلَى بَيْتِ اللَّهِ فَإِنْ أَبَى أَنْ يَحُجَّ مَعَهُ فَلَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ شَىْءٌ وَقَدْ قَضَى مَا عَلَيْهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى سُئِلَ مَالِكٌ عَنِ الرَّجُلِ يَحْلِفُ بِنُذُورٍ مُسَمَّاةٍ مَشْيًا إِلَى بَيْتِ اللَّهِ أَنْ لاَ يُكَلِّمَ أَخَاهُ أَوْ أَبَاهُ بِكَذَا وَكَذَا نَذْرًا لِشَىْءٍ لاَ يَقْوَى عَلَيْهِ وَلَوْ تَكَلَّفَ ذَلِكَ كُلَّ عَامٍ لَعُرِفَ أَنَّهُ لاَ يَبْلُغُ عُمْرُهُ مَا جَعَلَ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ مِنْ ذَلِكَ فَقِيلَ لَهُ هَلْ يُجْزِيهِ مِنْ ذَلِكَ نَذْرٌ وَاحِدٌ أَوْ نُذُورٌ مُسَمَّاةٌ فَقَالَ مَالِكٌ مَا أَعْلَمُهُ يُجْزِئُهُ مِنْ ذَلِكَ إِلاَّ الْوَفَاءُ بِمَا جَعَلَ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ فَلْيَمْشِ مَا قَدَرَ عَلَيْهِ مِنَ الزَّمَانِ وَلْيَتَقَرَّبْ إِلَى اللَّهِ تَعَالَى بِمَا اسْتَطَاعَ مِنَ الْخَيْرِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Yahya ibn Said (successor); Ata ibn Abi Rabah (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said said, &quot;I vowed to walk, but I was struck by a pain in the kidney, so I rode until I came to Makka. I questioned Ata ibn Abi Rabah and others, and they said, &#x27;You must sacrifice an animal.&#x27; When I came to Madina I questioned the ulama there, and they ordered me to walk again from the place from which I was unable to go on. So I walked.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1014]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1014" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1014</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1014 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Yahya said that he had heard Malik say, &quot;What is done among us regarding someone who makes a vow to walk to the House of Allah, and then cannot do it and so rides, is that he must return and walk from the place from which he was unable to go on. If he cannot walk, he should walk what he can and then ride, and he must sacrifice a camel, a cow, or a sheep if that is all that he can find.&quot; Malik, when asked about a man who said to another, &quot;I will carry you to the House of Allah&quot;, answered, &quot;If he intended to carry him on his shoulder, by that he meant hardship and exhaustion to himself, and he does not have to do that. Let him walk by foot and make sacrifice. If he did not intend anything, let him do hajj and ride, and take the man on hajj with him. That is because he said, &#x27;I will carry you to the house of Allah.&#x27; If the man refuses to do hajj with him, then there is nothing against him, and what is demanded of him is cancelled.&quot; Yahya said that Malik was asked whether it was enough for a man who had made a vow that he would walk to the House of Allah a certain (large) number of times, or who had forbidden himself from talking to his father and brother, if he did not fulfil a certain vow, and he had taken upon himself, by the oath, something which he was incapable of fulfilling in his lifetime, even though he were to try every year, to fulfil only one or a (smaller) number of vows by Allah? Malik said, &quot;The only satisfaction for that that I know is fulfilling what he has obliged himself to do. Let him walk for as long as he is able and draw near Allah the Exalted by what he can of good</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1014" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यहया बिन सईद ने कहा मैं ने बैतुल्लाह तक पैदल चलने की नज़्र की थी, मेरी नाफ़ में दर्द होने लगा मैं सवार हो कर मक्का में आया और अता बिन अबी रबाह वग़ैरा से पूछा उन्होंने कहा तुझ को हद्य लाज़िम है। जब मैं मदीना आया वहां लोगों से पूछा उन्होंने कहा तुझ को दोबारा पैदल चलना चाहिए जहां से सवार हुआ था तो पैदल चलाये। कहा मालिक ने हमारे नज़दीक जो शख़्स ये कहे के &#x27;मुझ पर पैदल चलना है बैतुल्लाह तक&#x27; और चले फिर आजिज़ हो जाये तो सवार हो जाये, फिर दोबारा जब आये तो जहां से सवार हुआ था वहां से पैदल चले, अगर चलने की ताक़त न हो तो जहां तक हो सके चले फिर सवार हो जाये और हद्य में एक ऊँट या गाये दे अगर न हो सके तो बकरी दे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1014" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحیی بن سعید نے کہا میں نے بیت اللہ تک پیدل چلنے کی نذر کی تھی میری ناف میں درد ہونے لگا میں سوار ہو کر مکے میں آیا اور عطا بن ابی رباح وغیرہ سے پوچھا انہوں نے کہا تجھ کو ہدی لازم ہے جب میں مدینہ آیا وہاں لوگوں سے پوچھا انہوں نے کہا تجھ کو دوبارہ پیدل چلنا چاہئے جہاں سے سوار ہوا تھا تو پیدل چلائیں ۔ کہا مالک نے ہمارے نزدیک جو شخص یہ کہے کہ مجھ پر پیدل چلنا ہے بیت اللہ تک اور چلے پھر عاجز ہو جائے تو سوار ہو جائے پھر دوبارہ جب آئے تو جہاں سے سوار ہوا تھا وہاں سے پیدل چلے اگر چلنے کی طاقت نہ ہو تو جہاں تک ہو سکے چلے پھر سوار ہو جائے اور ہدی میں ایک اونٹ یا گائے دے اگر نہ ہو سکے تو بکری دے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya bin Saeed ne kaha main ne bait-ullah tak paidal chalne ki nazr ki thi, meri naaf mein dard hone laga main sawar ho kar Makkah mein aaya aur Ata bin Abi Rabah wagera se poocha unhone kaha tujh ko hadya lazim hai. Jab main Madina aaya wahan logon se poocha unhone kaha tujh ko dobara paidal chalna chahiye jahan se sawar hua tha to paidal chalaye. Kaha Malik ne hamare nazdeek jo shakhs ye kahe ke &#x27;mujh par paidal chalna hai bait-ullah tak&#x27; aur chale phir aajiz ho jaye to sawar ho jaye, phir dobara jab aaye to jahan se sawar hua tha wahan se paidal chale, agar chalne ki taqat na ho to jahan tak ho sake chale phir sawar ho jaye aur hadya mein ek oont ya gaye de agar na ho sake to bakri de.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1014" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1008

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1015)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ حُمَيْدِ بْنِ قَيْسٍ، وَثَوْرِ بْنِ زَيْدٍ الدِّيلِيِّ، أَنَّهُمَا أَخْبَرَاهُ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَأَحَدُهُمَا يَزِيدُ فِي الْحَدِيثِ عَلَى صَاحِبِهِ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَأَى رَجُلاً قَائِمًا فِي الشَّمْسِ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَا بَالُ هَذَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا نَذَرَ أَنْ لاَ يَتَكَلَّمَ وَلاَ يَسْتَظِلَّ مِنَ الشَّمْسِ وَلاَ يَجْلِسَ وَيَصُومَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ مُرُوهُ فَلْيَتَكَلَّمْ وَلْيَسْتَظِلَّ وَلْيَجْلِسْ وَلْيُتِمَّ صِيَامَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلَمْ أَسْمَعْ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَمَرَهُ بِكَفَّارَةٍ وَقَدْ أَمَرَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنْ يُتِمَّ مَا كَانَ لِلَّهِ طَاعَةً وَيَتْرُكَ مَا كَانَ لِلَّهِ مَعْصِيَةً ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1015]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1015" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that what he preferred of what he had heard from the people of knowledge about a man or woman who vowed to walk to the House of Allah, was that they fulfilled the oath when performing umra, by walking until they had done say between Safa and Marwa. When they had done say it was finished. If they vowed to walk in the hajj, they walked until they came to Makka, then they walked until they had finished all the rites. Malik said, &quot;Walking is only for hajj or umra.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik that Humayd ibn Qays and Thawr ibn Zayd adDili both informed him that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, (and one of them gave more detail than the other),saw a man standing in the sun. The Messenger asked, &quot;What&#x27;s wrong with him?&quot; The people said, &quot;He has vowed not to speak or to seek shade from the sun or to sit and to fast.&quot; The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Go and tell him to speak, seek shade, and sit, but let him complete his fast.&quot; Malik said, &quot;I have not heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ordered the man in question to do any kaffara. The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, only ordered him to complete that in which there was obedience to Allah and to abandon that in which there was disobedience to Allah</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1015]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1015" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हुमैद बिन क़ैस और तौर बिन ज़ैद से रिवायत है के रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने एक शख़्स को धूप में खड़ा हुआ देखा, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उसका बाइसे पूछा। लोगों ने कहा इसने नज़्र की है के मैं किसी से बात न करूँगा, न साया लूँगा, न बैठूँगा और रोज़ा से रहूँगा। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के इसको हुक्म करो बात करे, साया में आये, बैठे, रोज़ा अपना पूरा करे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1015]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1015" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حمید بن قیس اور ثور بن زید سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ایک شخص کو دھوپ میں کھڑا ہوا دیکھا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اس کا باعث پوچھا لوگوں نے کہا اس نے نذر کی ہے کہ میں کسی سے بات نہ کروں گا نہ سایہ لوں گا نہ بیٹھوں گا اور روزہ سے رہوں گا۔ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا کہ اس کو حکم کرو بات کرے، سایہ میں آئے، بیٹھے، روزہ اپنا پورا کرے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1015]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Humaid bin Qais aur Thaur bin Zaid se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne ek shakhs ko dhoop mein khada hua dekha, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne uska baa&#x27;is poocha. Logon ne kaha isne nazr ki hai ke main kisi se baat na karunga, na saya loonga, na baithunga aur roza se rahunga. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya ke isko hukm karo baat kare, saya mein aaye, baithe, roza apna poora kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1015]</p>

## Narration 1009

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1016)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1016]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1016" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1016</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1016 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، أَنَّهُ سَمِعَهُ يَقُولُ أَتَتِ امْرَأَةٌ إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ فَقَالَتْ إِنِّي نَذَرْتُ أَنْ أَنْحَرَ ابْنِي ‏.‏ فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ لاَ تَنْحَرِي ابْنَكِ وَكَفِّرِي عَنْ يَمِينِكِ، ‏.‏ فَقَالَ شَيْخٌ عِنْدَ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ وَكَيْفَ يَكُونُ فِي هَذَا كَفَّارَةٌ فَقَالَ ابْنُ عَبَّاسٍ إِنَّ اللَّهَ تَعَالَى قَالَ وَ ‏{‏الَّذِينَ يُظَاهِرُونَ مِنْكُمْ مِنْ نِسَائِهِمْ‏}‏ ثُمَّ جَعَلَ فِيهِ مِنَ الْكَفَّارَةِ مَا قَدْ رَأَيْتَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1016" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Abbas (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that Yahya ibn Said heard al-Qasim ibn Muhammad say, &quot;A woman came to Abdullah ibn Abbas and said, &#x27;I have vowed to sacrifice my son.&#x27; Ibn Abbas said, &#x27;Do not sacrifice your son. Do kaffara for your oath.&#x27; An old man with Ibn Abbas said, &#x27;What kaffara is there for this?&#x27; Ibn Abbas said, &#x27;Allah the Exalted said, &quot;Those of you who say, regarding their wives.&#x27;Be as my mother&#x27;s back&#x27; (Sura58 ayat 2) and then He went on to oblige the kaffara for it as you have seen</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1016]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1016" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद से रिवायत है के एक औरत अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास आयी और बोली मैं ने नज़र की अपने बेटे को ज़िबह करने की. इब्न अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा मत ज़िबह कर अपने बेटे को और कफ़्फ़ारा दे अपनी क़सम का. एक शख्स बोलै इब्न अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से इस नज़र में कफ़्फ़ारा क्यूँ होगा? इब्न अब्बास (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने जवाब दिया के ज़िहार भी एक माँ&#x27;आसियत है और इस में अल्लाह ने कफ़्फ़ारा मुकर्रर किया.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1016" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد سے روایت ہے کہ ایک عورت عبداللہ بن عباس کے پاس آئی اور بولی میں نے نذر کی اپنے بیٹے کو ذبح کرنے کی ابن عباس نے کہا مت ذبح کر اپنے بیٹے کو اور کفارہ دے اپنی قسم کا ایک شخص بولا ابن عباس رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہما سے اس نذر میں کفارہ کیوں ہوگا ابن عباس نے جواب دیا کہ ظہار بھی ایک معصیت ہے اور اس میں اللہ نے کفارہ مقرر کیا ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Qasim bin Muhammad se riwayat hai ke ek aurat Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas aayi aur boli main ne nazr ki apne bete ko zibah karne ki. Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha mat zibah kar apne bete ko aur kaffara de apni qasam ka. Ek shakhs bola Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se is nazr mein kaffara kyun hoga? Ibn Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne jawab diya ke zihar bhi ek ma&#x27;asiyat hai aur is mein Allah ne kaffara muqarrar kiya.</p>

## Narration 1010

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1017)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ طَلْحَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْمَلِكِ الأَيْلِيِّ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ الصِّدِّيقِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ نَذَرَ أَنْ يُطِيعَ اللَّهَ فَلْيُطِعْهُ وَمَنْ نَذَرَ أَنْ يَعْصِيَ اللَّهَ فَلاَ يَعْصِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1017]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1017" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1017</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1017 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Talha ibn Abi al-Malik al- Ayli from al-Qasim ibn Muhammad ibn as-Siddiq from A&#x27;isha that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Whoever vows to obey Allah, let him obey Him. Whoever vows to disobey Allah, let him not disobey Him.&quot; Yahya said that he had heard Malik say, &quot;The meaning of the statement of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, &#x27;Whoever vows to disobey Allah, let him not disobey Him&#x27; is that for instance a man who vows that, if he speaks to such-and-such a person, he will walk to Syria, Egypt, or any other such things which are not considered as ibada, is not under any obligation by any of that, even if he did speak to the man or did break whatever it was he swore, because Allah does not demand obedience in such things. He should only fulfill those things in which there is obedience to Allah.&quot; 22.5 Rashness in Oaths</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1017]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1017" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1017</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1017 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">कहा मालिक ने रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने जो ये फ़रमाया &#x27;अगर कोई नज़्र करे अल्लाह की मासियत की तो मासियत न करे&#x27;, मुराद इस से ये है के मिसाल के तौर पर आदमी नज़्र करे शाम या मिस्र या जेद्दा या ज़ुबदा में जाने की या और किसी काम की जो सवाब नहीं है। अगर ऐसे उमूरत में उसकी क़सम टूटे मिसाल के तौर पर यूँ कहे के &#x27;अगर मैं ज़ैद से बात करूँ तो मिस्र जाऊँगा&#x27; फिर ज़ैद से बात करे तो उस पर कुछ लाज़िम नहीं आता बल्कि इस नज़्र का पूरा करना ज़रूर है जिस में सवाब हो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1017]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">کہا مالک نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے جو یہ فرمایا اگر کوئی نذر کرے اللہ کی معصیت کی تو معصیت نہ کرے مراد اس سے یہ ہے کہ مثلا آدمی نذر کرے شام یا مصر یا جدہ یا زبدہ میں جانے کی یا اور کسی کام کی جو ثواب نہیں ہے اگر ایسے امورات میں اس کی قسم ٹوٹے مثلا یوں کہے کہ اگر میں زید سے بات کروں تو مصر جاؤں گا پھر زید سے پات کرے تو اس پر کچھ لازم نہیں آتا بلکہ اس نذر کا پورا کرنا ضرور ہے جس میں ثواب ہو۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1017]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1017" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Kaha Malik ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne jo ye farmaya &#x27;agar koi nazr kare Allah ki ma&#x27;asiyat ki to ma&#x27;asiyat na kare&#x27;, murad is se ye hai ke misaal ke taur par aadmi nazr kare Shaam ya Misr ya Jeddah ya Zubdah mein jaane ki ya aur kisi kaam ki jo sawab nahi hai. Agar aise umoorat mein uski qasam toote misaal ke taur par yun kahe ke &#x27;agar main Zaid se baat karoon to Misr jaunga&#x27; phir Zaid se baat kare to us par kuch lazim nahi aata balki is nazr ka poora karna zaroor hai jis mein sawab ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1017]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1017" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1011

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1018]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1018" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1018</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1018 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، أَنَّهَا كَانَتْ تَقُولُ لَغْوُ الْيَمِينِ قَوْلُ الإِنْسَانِ لاَ وَاللَّهِ بَلَى وَاللَّهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ أَحْسَنُ مَا سَمِعْتُ فِي هَذَا أَنَّ اللَّغْوَ حَلِفُ الإِنْسَانِ عَلَى الشَّىْءِ يَسْتَيْقِنُ أَنَّهُ كَذَلِكَ ثُمَّ يُوجَدُ عَلَى غَيْرِ ذَلِكَ فَهُوَ اللَّغْوُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَعَقْدُ الْيَمِينِ أَنْ يَحْلِفَ الرَّجُلُ أَنْ لاَ يَبِيعَ ثَوْبَهُ بِعَشَرَةِ دَنَانِيرَ ثُمَّ يَبِيعَهُ بِذَلِكَ أَوْ يَحْلِفَ لَيَضْرِبَنَّ غُلاَمَهُ ثُمَّ لاَ يَضْرِبُهُ وَنَحْوَ هَذَا فَهَذَا الَّذِي يُكَفِّرُ صَاحِبُهُ عَنْ يَمِينِهِ وَلَيْسَ فِي اللَّغْوِ كَفَّارَةٌ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَأَمَّا الَّذِي يَحْلِفُ عَلَى الشَّىْءِ وَهُوَ يَعْلَمُ أَنَّهُ آثِمٌ وَيَحْلِفُ عَلَى الْكَذِبِ وَهُوَ يَعْلَمُ لِيُرْضِيَ بِهِ أَحَدًا أَوْ لِيَعْتَذِرَ بِهِ إِلَى مُعْتَذَرٍ إِلَيْهِ أَوْ لِيَقْطَعَ بِهِ مَالاً فَهَذَا أَعْظَمُ مِنْ أَنْ تَكُونَ فِيهِ كَفَّارَةٌ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1018" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: A&#x27;isha (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa from his father that A&#x27;isha, umm al-muminin said, &quot;Rashness in oaths is that a man says, &#x27;By Allah, No! by Allah!&#x27; &quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1018]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1018" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1018</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1018 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> i.e. out of habit. Malik said, &quot;The best of what I have heard on the matter is that rashness in oaths is that a man take an oath on something to show that he is certain that it is like he said, only to find that it is other than what he said. This is rashness.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The binding oath is for example, that a man says that he will not sell his garment for ten dinars, and then he sells it for that, or that he will beat his young slave and then does not beat him, and so on. One does kaffara for making such an oath, and there is no kaffara in rashness.&quot; Malik said, &quot;As for the one who swears to a thing which he knows is wicked, and he swears to a lie he knows to be a lie, in order to please someone with it or to excuse himself to someone by it or to gain money by it, no kaffara that he does for it can cover it</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1018" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ مَنْ قَالَ وَاللَّهِ ثُمَّ قَالَ إِنْ شَاءَ اللَّهُ ثُمَّ لَمْ يَفْعَلِ الَّذِي حَلَفَ عَلَيْهِ لَمْ يَحْنَثْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ أَحْسَنُ مَا سَمِعْتُ فِي الثُّنْيَا أَنَّهَا لِصَاحِبِهَا مَا لَمْ يَقْطَعْ كَلاَمَهُ وَمَا كَانَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ نَسَقًا يَتْبَعُ بَعْضُهُ بَعْضًا قَبْلَ أَنْ يَسْكُتَ فَإِذَا سَكَتَ وَقَطَعَ كَلاَمَهُ فَلاَ ثُنْيَا لَهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ يَحْيَى وَقَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الرَّجُلِ يَقُولُ كَفَرَ بِاللَّهِ أَوْ أَشْرَكَ بِاللَّهِ ثُمَّ يَحْنَثُ إِنَّهُ لَيْسَ عَلَيْهِ كَفَّارَةٌ وَلَيْسَ بِكَافِرٍ وَلاَ مُشْرِكٍ حَتَّى يَكُونَ قَلْبُهُ مُضْمِرًا عَلَى الشِّرْكِ وَالْكُفْرِ وَلْيَسْتَغْفِرِ اللَّهَ وَلاَ يَعُدْ إِلَى شَىْءٍ مِنْ ذَلِكَ وَبِئْسَ مَا صَنَعَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1019" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;Whoever swears by Allah and then says, &#x27;Allah willing&#x27; and then does not do what he has sworn to, has not broken his oath.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1019]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1019" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1019</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1019 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The best I have heard on this reservation is that it belongs to the statement made if the speaker does not break the normal flow of speech before he is silent. If he is silent and breaks the flow of speech, he has no exception.&quot; Yahya said, &quot;Malik said that a man who said that he had disbelieved or associated something with Allah and then he broke his oath, had no kaffara, and he was not a disbeliever or one who associated something with Allah unless his heart concealed something of either of those. He should ask forgiveness of Allah and not return to it - for what he did was evil</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1019" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1013

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1020)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ سُهَيْلِ بْنِ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ مَنْ حَلَفَ بِيَمِينٍ فَرَأَى غَيْرَهَا خَيْرًا مِنْهَا فَلْيُكَفِّرْ عَنْ يَمِينِهِ وَلْيَفْعَلِ الَّذِي هُوَ خَيْرٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1020]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1020" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1020</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1020 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Suhayl ibn Abi Salih from his father from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Whoever makes an oath and then sees that something else would be better than it, should do kaffara for his oath and do what is better.&quot; Yahya said that he heard Malik say, &quot;Anyone who says that he has a vow but does not mention the name of Allah, is still obliged to make the kaffara for an oath (if he breaks it)&quot;. Malik said, &quot;Emphasis is when a man swears one thing several times, repeating the oath in his speech time after time. For instance, the statement, &#x27;By Allah, I will not decrease it from such-and-such,&#x27; sworn three times or more. The kaffara of that is like the kaffara of one oath. If a man swears, &#x27;I will not eat this food or wear these clothes or enter this house,&#x27; that is all in one oath, and he is only obliged to do one kaffara. It is the same for a man who says to his wife, &#x27;You are divorced if I clothe you in this garment or let you go to the mosque,&#x27; and it is one entire statement in the normal pattern of speech. If he breaks any of that oath, divorce is necessary, and there is no breaking of oath after that in whatever he does. There is only one oath to be broken in that.&quot; Malik said, &quot;What we do about a woman who makes a vow without her husband&#x27;s permission is that she is allowed to do so and she must fulfill it, if it only concerns her own person and will not harm her husband. If, however, it will harm her husband, he may forbid her to fulfill it, but it remains an obligation against her until she has the opportunity to complete it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1020]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1020" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया जो शख्स क़सम खाये किसी काम पर फिर उसके खिलाफ बेहतर मालूम हो तो कफ़्फ़ारा दे क़सम का और करे जो बेहतर मालूम हो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1020]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1020" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1014

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1021)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1021]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1021" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1021</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1021 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يَقُولُ مَنْ حَلَفَ بِيَمِينٍ فَوَكَّدَهَا ثُمَّ حَنِثَ فَعَلَيْهِ عِتْقُ رَقَبَةٍ أَوْ كِسْوَةُ عَشَرَةِ مَسَاكِينَ وَمَنْ حَلَفَ بِيَمِينٍ فَلَمْ يُؤَكِّدْهَا ثُمَّ حَنِثَ فَعَلَيْهِ إِطْعَامُ عَشَرَةِ مَسَاكِينَ لِكُلِّ مِسْكِينٍ مُدٌّ مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ فَمَنْ لَمْ يَجِدْ فَصِيَامُ ثَلاَثَةِ أَيَّامٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1021" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;If someone breaks an oath which he has stressed, he has to free a slave, or clothe ten poor people. If someone breaks an oath, but has not stressed it, he only has to feed ten poor people and each poor person is fed a mudd of wheat. Some one who does not have the means for that, should fast for three days</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1021]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1021" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहा करते थे कि जो शख़्स क़सम खाए, फिर उसे बार बार दोहराए और फिर क़सम तोड़े, तो उस पर एक ग़ुलाम आज़ाद करना या दस मिस्कीनों को कपड़े पहनाना लाज़िम होगा। अगर सिर्फ़ एक मर्तबा क़सम खाई हो तो दस मिस्कीनों को खाना खिलाए, हर मिस्कीन को एक मुद्द गेहूं दे, और अगर इस पर क़ुदरत न हो तो तीन रोज़े रखे।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1021" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر کہتے تھے جو شخص قسم کھائے پھر اس کو مکرر سہ کرر کہے پھر قسم توڑے تو اس پر ایک بردے کا آزاد کرنا یا دس مسکینوں کو کپڑا پہنانا لازم آئے گا اگر ایک ہی مرتبہ کہے تو دس مسکینوں کو کھانا دے ہر مسکین کو ایک مد گہیوں کا اگر اس پر قدرت نہ ہو تو تین روزے رکھے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) kaha karte the ki jo shakhs qasam khaye, phir usay bar bar duhraiye aur phir qasam tode, to us par ek ghulam azad karna ya das miskeeno ko kapde pehnana lazim hoga. Agar sirf ek martaba qasam khai ho to das miskeeno ko khana khilaye, har miskeen ko ek mudd gehu de, aur agar is par qudrat na ho to teen rozay rakhe.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1021" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ كَانَ يُكَفِّرُ عَنْ يَمِينِهِ، بِإِطْعَامِ عَشَرَةِ مَسَاكِينَ لِكُلِّ مِسْكِينٍ مُدٌّ مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ وَكَانَ يَعْتِقُ الْمِرَارَ إِذَا وَكَّدَ الْيَمِينَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1022" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that Abdullah ibn Umar used to do kaffara for a broken oath by feeding ten poor people. Each person got a mudd of wheat. He sometimes freed a slave if he had repeated the oath</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1022]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1022" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1016

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1023)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1023]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1023</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1023 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ أَدْرَكْتُ النَّاسَ وَهُمْ إِذَا أَعْطَوْا فِي كَفَّارَةِ الْيَمِينِ أَعْطَوْا مُدًّا مِنْ حِنْطَةٍ بِالْمُدِّ الأَصْغَرِ وَرَأَوْا ذَلِكَ مُجْزِئًا عَنْهُمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ أَحْسَنُ مَا سَمِعْتُ فِي الَّذِي يُكَفِّرُ عَنْ يَمِينِهِ بِالْكِسْوَةِ أَنَّهُ إِنْ كَسَا الرِّجَالَ كَسَاهُمْ ثَوْبًا ثَوْبًا وَإِنْ كَسَا النِّسَاءَ كَسَاهُنَّ ثَوْبَيْنِ ثَوْبَيْنِ دِرْعًا وَخِمَارًا وَذَلِكَ أَدْنَى مَا يُجْزِي كُلاًّ فِي صَلاَتِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Sulayman ibn Yasar (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that Sulayman ibn Yasar said, &quot;I understood from people that when they made the kaffara for a broken oath, they gave a mudd of wheat according to the smaller mudd. They thought that that would compensate for them.&quot;</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1023]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1023</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1023 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The best of what I have heard about the one who does kaffara for breaking his oath by clothing people is that if he clothes men he clothes them each in one garment. If he clothes women, he clothes them each in two garments, a long shift and a long scarf, because that is what is satisfactory for each of them in the prayer</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1023" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1017

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1024)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَدْرَكَ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ - رضى الله عنه - وَهُوَ يَسِيرُ فِي رَكْبٍ وَهُوَ يَحْلِفُ بِأَبِيهِ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّ اللَّهَ يَنْهَاكُمْ أَنْ تَحْلِفُوا بِآبَائِكُمْ فَمَنْ كَانَ حَالِفًا فَلْيَحْلِفْ بِاللَّهِ أَوْ لِيَصْمُتْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1024</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1024 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi from Abdullah ibn Umar that one time the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was speaking to Umar ibn al-Khattab while he was travelling with a troop and Umar swore by his father and he (the Messenger) said, &quot;Allah forbids you to swear by your fathers. If anyone swears, let him swear by Allah or keep silent</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1024</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1024 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) उमर बिन खत्ताब से मिले जब वो सवारों में जा रहे थे और अपने बाप की क़सम खा रहे थे. आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: अल्लाह (जल्ला जलालहु) तुम्हे अपने बाप की क़सम खाने से मन करता है खाएं या फिर चुप रहे. इमाम मालिक तक ये बात पहुँची कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) &#x27;मुक़ल्लिब-उल-क़ुलूब&#x27; की क़सम फ़रमाया करते थे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ملے عمر بن خطاب سے اور وہ جا رہے تھے سواروں میں اور قسم کھا رہے تھے اپنے باپ کی فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اللہ جل جلالہ منع کرتا ہے تم کو اس بات سے کہ قسم کھاؤ تم اپنے باپو کی جو شخص تم میں سے قسم کھانی چاہے تو اللہ کی قسم کھائے یا چپ رہے ۔ امام مالک کو پہنچا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم فرماتے تھے قسم مقلب القلوب کی ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1024]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1024" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) Umar bin Khattab se mile jab wo sawaron mein ja rahe the aur apne baap ki qasam kha rahe the. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Allah (Jalla Jalaluhu) tumhe apne baap ki qasam khane se mana karta hai, jo shakhs qasam khana chahe wo Allah ki qasam khaye ya phir chup rahe. Imam Malik tak ye baat pahunchi ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) &#x27;Muqallib-ul-Quloob&#x27; ki qasam farmaya karte the.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1024]</p>

## Narration 1018

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1025)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَانَ يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ وَمُقَلِّبِ الْقُلُوبِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1025]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1025" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, used tosay, &quot;No, by the Overturner of hearts</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1025]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1025" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1025]</p>

## Narration 1019

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1026)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ حَفْصِ بْنِ عُمَرَ بْنِ خَلْدَةَ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ أَبَا لُبَابَةَ بْنَ عَبْدِ الْمُنْذِرِ، حِينَ تَابَ اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ قَالَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ أَهْجُرُ دَارَ قَوْمِي الَّتِي أَصَبْتُ فِيهَا الذَّنْبَ وَأُجَاوِرُكَ وَأَنْخَلِعُ مِنْ مَالِي صَدَقَةً إِلَى اللَّهِ وَإِلَى رَسُولِهِ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ يُجْزِيكَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ الثُّلُثُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1026</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1026 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Uthman ibn Hafs ibn Umar ibn Khalda that Ibn Shihab had heard that Abu Lubaba ibn Abd al-Mundhir, when Allah turned to him said, &quot;Messenger of Allah, should I leave my people&#x27;s house in which I committed wrong action and keep your company, and give away all my property as sadaqa for Allah and His Messenger? &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Giving away a third of it is enough for you</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1026</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1026 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">इब्न शिहाब से रिवायत है की जब अल्लाह ने अबू लुबाबा की तोबा क़बूल की तो उन्होंने कहा अलैहि वसल्लम)! क्या मैं अपनी क़ौम के घर को छोड़ दूँ जहाँ मैंने गुनाह किया था और आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के करीब रहूँ और अपने माल में से अल्लाह और उसके रसूल (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के लिए सदक़ा निकलूं? तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: तेरा एक तिहाई (१/३) माल सदक़ा निकलना काफी है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1026 ]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابن شہاب سے روایت ہے کہ ابولبابہ کی توبہ جب اللہ نے قبول کی تو انہوں نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کیا چھوڑ دوں میں اپنی قوم کے گھر کو جس میں میں نے گناہ کیا اور آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے قریب رہوں اور اپنے مال میں سے صدقہ نکالوں اللہ ورسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے واسطے تو فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے تہائی مال تجھ کو اپنے مال میں سے صدقہ نکالنا کافی ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1026]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1026" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Ibn Shihab se riwayat hai ki jab Allah ne Abu Lubaba ki toba qabool ki to unhone kaha, Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam)! Kya main apni qoum ke ghar ko chhor doon jahan maine gunah kiya tha aur aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke qareeb rahoon aur apne maal mein se Allah aur uske Rasool (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke liye sadqa nikaloon? To aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Tera ek tihai (1/3) maal sadqa nikalna kafi hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1026]</p>

## Narration 1020

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1027)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 22</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ بْنِ مُوسَى، عَنْ مَنْصُورِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ الْحَجَبِيِّ، عَنْ أُمِّهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ أُمِّ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ، - رضى الله عنها أَنَّهَا سُئِلَتْ عَنْ رَجُلٍ قَالَ مَالِي فِي رِتَاجِ الْكَعْبَةِ ‏.‏ فَقَالَتْ عَائِشَةُ يُكَفِّرُهُ مَا يُكَفِّرُ الْيَمِينَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فِي الَّذِي يَقُولُ مَالِي فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ ثُمَّ يَحْنَثُ قَالَ يَجْعَلُ ثُلُثَ مَالِهِ فِي سَبِيلِ اللَّهِ وَذَلِكَ لِلَّذِي جَاءَ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فِي أَمْرِ أَبِي لُبَابَةَ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1027]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1027" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>
