<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ayyub ibn Musa from Mansur ibn Abd ar-Rahman al-Hajabi from his mother that A&#x27;isha, umm al- muminin, may Allah be pleased with her, was asked about a man who devoted his property to the door of Kaba. She said, &quot;Let him do kaffara for it with the kaffara of the oath.&quot; Malik said, that someone who devoted all his property in the way of Allah, and then broke his oath, should put a third of his property in the way of Allah, as that was what the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, did in the case of Abu Lubaba</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1027]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1027" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रत आयशा (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हा) से सवाल हुआ, एक शख़्स ने कहा मेरा माल काबा के दरवाज़े पर वक़्फ़ है, उन्होंने फ़रमाया इस पर क़सम का कफ़्फ़ारा लाज़िम होगा। मालिक ने कहा कि जो शख़्स ये कहे कि मेरा माल अल्लाह की राह में है, तो वो एक तिहाई माल सदक़ा करे क्योंकि आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अबू लुबाबा को ऐसा ही हुक्म दिया था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1027]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1027" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ سے سوال ہوا ایک شخص نے کہا مال میرا کعبہ کے دروازے پر وقف ہے انہوں نے کہا اس میں کفارہ قسم کا لازم آئے گا ۔ کہا مالک نے جو شخص یہ کہے کہ مال میرا اللہ کی راہ میں ہے تو تہائی مال صدقہ کرے کیونکہ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے ابی لبابہ کو ایسا ہی حکم کیا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1027]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) se sawal hua, ek shakhs ne kaha mera maal Kaaba ke darwaze par waqf hai, unhone farmaya is par qasam ka kaffara lazim hoga. Malik ne kaha ki jo shakhs ye kahe ki mera maal Allah ki raah mein hai, to wo ek tihai maal sadqa kare kyunki aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne Abu Lubaba ko aisa hi hukum diya tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1027]</p>

## Narration 1021

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1028)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 23</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَمْرِو بْنِ الْحَارِثِ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ بْنِ فَيْرُوزَ، عَنِ الْبَرَاءِ بْنِ عَازِبٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم سُئِلَ مَاذَا يُتَّقَى مِنَ الضَّحَايَا فَأَشَارَ بِيَدِهِ وَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَرْبَعًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَكَانَ الْبَرَاءُ يُشِيرُ بِيَدِهِ وَيَقُولُ يَدِي أَقْصَرُ مِنْ يَدِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ الْعَرْجَاءُ الْبَيِّنُ ظَلْعُهَا وَالْعَوْرَاءُ الْبَيِّنُ عَوَرُهَا وَالْمَرِيضَةُ الْبَيِّنُ مَرَضُهَا وَالْعَجْفَاءُ الَّتِي لاَ تُنْقِي ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1028]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1028" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1028</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1028 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Amr ibn al-Harith from Ubayd ibn Fayruz from al-Bara ibn Azib that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was asked what animals should be avoided as sacrifices. He indicated with his hand and said, &quot;Four.&quot; Al-Bara pointed with his hand and said, &quot;My hand is shorter than the hand of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. A lame animal whose lameness is evident, a one-eyed animal which is clearly one-eyed, an animal which is clearly ill, and an emaciated animal with no fat on it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1028]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1028" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1028</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1028 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">ब़रा बिन आज़िब (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत है कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से पूछा गया कि कुर्बानी में किन जानवरों से बचना चाहिए? आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने अपनी उंगलियों से इशारा करके बताया कि चार से बचना चाहिए। ब़रा बिन आज़िब भी उंगलियों से इशारा करते और कहते कि मेरा हाथ रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के हाथ से छोटा है। एक लंगड़ा जानवर जो चल न सके, एक काना जिसका कानापन ज़ाहिर हो, एक बीमार जिसकी बीमारी ज़ाहिर हो, और एक दुबला जिसमें गुदा (मज्जा) न हो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1028]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1028" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">براء بن عازب سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے پوچھا گیا قربانی میں کن جانوروں سے بچنا چاہئے؟ آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اپنی انگلیوں سے بتایا کہ چار سے بچنا چاہئے براء بن عازب بھی انگلیوں سے بتایا کرتے اور کہتے کہ میرا ہاتھ چوڑا ہے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کے ہاتھ سے، ایک لنگڑا جو چل نہ سکے اور کانا جس کا کانا پن کھلا ہو اور بیمار جس کی بیماری ظاہر ہوا اور دبلا جس میں گودا نہیں ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1028]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1028" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Bara bin Azib (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se pucha gaya ki qurbani mein kin janwaron se bachna chahiye? Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne apni ungliyon se ishara karke bataya ki char se bachna chahiye. Bara bin Azib bhi ungliyon se ishara karte aur kehte ki mera haath Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke haath se chota hai. Ek langda janwar jo chal na sake, ek kana jiska kanapan zahir ho, ek beemar jiski beemari zahir ho, aur ek dubla jisme guda (majja) na ho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1028]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1028" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1023

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1030)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 23</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1030]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1030" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1030</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1030 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ نَافِعٍ، أَنَّ عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ عُمَرَ، ضَحَّى مَرَّةً بِالْمَدِينَةِ ‏.‏ قَالَ نَافِعٌ فَأَمَرَنِي أَنْ أَشْتَرِيَ لَهُ كَبْشًا فَحِيلاً أَقْرَنَ ثُمَّ أَذْبَحَهُ يَوْمَ الأَضْحَى فِي مُصَلَّى النَّاسِ ‏.‏ قَالَ نَافِعٌ فَفَعَلْتُ ثُمَّ حُمِلَ إِلَى عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ فَحَلَقَ رَأْسَهُ حِينَ ذُبِحَ الْكَبْشُ وَكَانَ مَرِيضًا لَمْ يَشْهَدِ الْعِيدَ مَعَ النَّاسِ ‏.‏ قَالَ نَافِعٌ وَكَانَ عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ يَقُولُ لَيْسَ حِلاَقُ الرَّأْسِ بِوَاجِبٍ عَلَى مَنْ ضَحَّى ‏.‏ وَقَدْ فَعَلَهُ ابْنُ عُمَرَ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1030" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This source entry preserves two separately attributed reports in their original order. They remain one provider entry, with their distinct narrator introductions and source references. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that one time Abdullah ibn Umar wanted to sacrifice an animal at Madina. Nafi said, &quot;He told me to buy him an excellent horned ram, then to sacrifice it on the Day of Sacrifice in the place where the people prayed.&quot; Nafi continued, &quot;I did so and when the ram was sacrificed, it was carried to Abdullah ibn Umar who shaved his head. He was ill, and did not attend the Id with the people.&quot;</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 2</p>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi added, &quot;Abdullah ibn Umar used to say, &#x27;Shaving the head is not obligatory for someone who sacrifices an animal.&#x27; Ibn Umar would do so however</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1030]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1030" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">नफ़ी से रिवायत है कि अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने एक बार मदीना में क़ुरबानी की तो मुझे हुकुम दिया की एक सिंह वाला बकरा खरीदू और ईद-उल-ाधा के दिन ईद-गाह में ज़िबह करून. मैंने ऐसा ही किया बकरा अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास भेजा गया जब उन्होंने अपना सर मुंडवाया. उन दिनों वो बीमार थे और ईद की नमाज़ के लिए भी नहीं आ सके थे. नफ़ी ने कहा कि अब्दुल्लाह बिन उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते थे कि सर मुंडवाना क़ुरबानी करने वाले पर वाजिब नहीं है</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1030" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">نافع سے روایت ہے کہ عبداللہ بن عمر نے قربانی کی ایک بار مدینہ میں تو مجھ کو حکم کیا ایک بکرا سینگ دار خریدنے کا اور اس کے ذبح کرنے کا عیدالاضحی کے روز عیدگاہ میں میں نے ایسا ہی کیا پھر وہ بکرا ذبح کیا ہوا بیجھا گیا عبداللہ بن عمر کے پاس جب انہوں نے اپنا سر منڈایا، ان دنوں میں وہ بیمار تھے عید کی نماز کو بھی نہیں آئے کہا نافع نے عبداللہ بن عمر کہتے تھے کہ سر منڈانا قربانی کرنے والے پر واجب نہیں ہے مگر عبداللہ بن عمر نے یوں ہی سر منڈایا ۔</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1030]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1030" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1030</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1030 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Nafi se riwayat hai ki Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ek baar Madina mein qurbani ki to mujhe hukum diya ki ek singh wala bakra kharidoo aur Eid-ul-Adha ke din Eid-gah mein zibah karoon. Maine aisa hi kiya, phir wo zibah shuda bakra Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas bheja gaya jab unhone apna sar mundwaya. Un dino wo beemar the aur Eid ki namaz ke liye bhi nahi aa sake the. Nafi ne kaha ki Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte the ki sar mundwana qurbani karne wale par wajib nahi hai, magar unhone khud aisa kiya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1030" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1024

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1031)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 23</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنْ بُشَيْرِ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّ أَبَا بُرْدَةَ بْنَ نِيَارٍ، ذَبَحَ ضَحِيَّتَهُ قَبْلَ أَنْ يَذْبَحَ، رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَوْمَ الأَضْحَى فَزَعَمَ أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَمَرَهُ أَنْ يَعُودَ بِضَحِيَّةٍ أُخْرَى ‏.‏ قَالَ أَبُو بُرْدَةَ لاَ أَجِدُ إِلاَّ جَذَعًا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ ‏.‏ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ وَإِنْ لَمْ تَجِدْ إِلاَّ جَذَعًا فَاذْبَحْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1031]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1031" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1031</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1031 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">ahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Bushayr ibn Yasar that Abu Burda ibn Niyar sacrificed an animal before the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, sacrificed on the Day of Sacrifice. He asserted that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ordered him to sacrifice another animal, and he, Abu Burda, said, &quot;What if I can only find an animal less than one year old, Messenger of Allah?&quot; He had said, &quot;If you can only find a young animal, then sacrifice it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1031]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1031" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">बशीर बिन यसार से रिवायत है के अबू बुर्दह बिन नियार ने अपनी कुर्बानी रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) के ज़िबह करने से पहले ही ज़िबह कर ली थी, तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने उन्हें दोबारा कुर्बानी करने का हुकुम दिया। उन्होंने अर्ज़ किया: या रसूलुल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम), मेरे पास तो अब कुछ नहीं सिर्फ एक साल की बकरी है। आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फरमाया: इसी को ज़िबह कर लो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1031]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1031" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">بشیر بن یسار سے روایت ہے کہ ابا بردہ بن نیار نے ذبح کی قربانی اپنی قبل اس بات کے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم ذبح کریں تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے دوسری قربانی کا ان کو حکم دیا انہوں نے کہا کہ یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم میرے پاس تو اب کچھ نہیں صرف ایک بکری ہے ایک سال کی آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا اسی کو ذبح کر ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1031]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Bashir bin Yasar se riwayat hai ke Abu Burdah bin Niyar ne apni qurbani Rasulullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ke zibah karne se pehle hi zibah kar li thi, to Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne unhe dobara qurbani karne ka hukum diya. Unhone arz kiya: Ya Rasulullah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam), mere paas to ab kuch nahi sirf ek saal ki bakri hai. Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Isi ko zibah kar lo.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1031]</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said from Abbad ibn Tamin that one time Uwaymir ibn Ashqar sacrificed his animal before the prayer on the morning of the Day of Sacrifice, and he mentioned that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, had ordered him to sacrifice another animal</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1032]</p>

<p dir="ltr">Ubada bin Tamim se riwayat hai ke Uwaimir bin Ashqar ne apni qurbani 10 Zil-Hajj ki fajr se pehle hi zibah kar li thi, jab unhone Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se ye bayan kiya to Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne unhe dobara qurbani karne ka hukum diya.</p>
